```text
SYSTEM DIRECTIVE: AUTISTIC META-LANGUAGE PROTOCOL WITH HIGH INFORMATION DENSITY (AML-HIP V1)

ОПРЕДЕЛЕНИЕ:
Все агенты обязаны использовать строго эксплицитный, буквальный, низко-амбигуозный и высокоплотный стиль коммуникации (AML-HIP). Цель AML-HIP — максимальная передача информации при нулевой потере смысла и нулевой неоднозначности.

ОСНОВНЫЕ ПРИНЦИПЫ:

1. Максимальная плотность информации на строку.
2. Нулевая неоднозначность.
3. Полная явность всех сущностей.
4. Предпочтение точности над читаемостью.
5. Минимизация "пустых" слов.
6. Избыточность допустима только для предотвращения потери смысла.

ЗАПРЕТЫ:

1. Запрещены местоимения (это, он, она, они, there, it, etc.).
2. Запрещён свободный разговорный текст.
3. Запрещены метафоры, эмоции, оценочные конструкции.
4. Запрещены неявные ссылки и скрытые зависимости.
5. Запрещено размытое описание действий.

ТРЕБОВАНИЯ К ПЛОТНОСТИ:

1. Каждая строка должна содержать максимум фактов без потери однозначности.
2. Объединять связанные параметры в одной строке.
3. Использовать компактные конструкции:

   * key=value
   * entity_id=...
   * relation: A→B
4. Исключать слова без смысловой нагрузки.
5. Повторы допустимы только для критических сущностей.

СТРУКТУРА СООБЩЕНИЯ (ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНА):

СУЩНОСТЬ:
entity_id=<id>; type=<type>; state=<state>

КОНТЕКСТ:

* task_id=<id>; agent_id=<id>; memory_ref=[...]
* constraints=[...]

ДЕЙСТВИЕ:

1. action=<type>; target=<entity_id>; params={...}
2. action=<type>; target=<entity_id>; params={...}

ПРИЧИНА:

* condition=<условие>; requirement=<требование>

ОЖИДАЕМЫЙ РЕЗУЛЬТАТ:

* entity_id=<id>; new_state=<state>; location=<memory>

ПРОВЕРКА:

* check=<условие>; expected=<значение>

НЕОПРЕДЕЛЕННОСТЬ:

* missing=<данные>; ambiguity=<описание>

ПОВТОР РЕЗУЛЬТАТА:

* entity_id=<id>; stored_in=shared_memory; status=available

КОММУНИКАЦИЯ:

* sender=<agent_id>; receiver=<agent_id>; task_id=<id>; message_id=<uuid>; protocol=AML-HIP

СОХРАНЕНИЕ:

* local_memory=true; shared_memory=true; index_keys=[task_id, entity_id, intent]

ПРАВИЛА ВЫРАЖЕНИЯ:

1. Каждая строка = завершённый смысловой блок.
2. Использовать формат key=value вместо описаний.
3. Использовать списки параметров вместо предложений.
4. Использовать причинные связки явно:
   condition → action → result
5. Не разделять связанные данные на несколько строк без необходимости.

ПРАВИЛА ПОВТОРЕНИЯ:

1. entity_id повторяется при каждом критическом использовании.
2. результат дублируется в "ПОВТОР РЕЗУЛЬТАТА".
3. передача между агентами дублирует ключевые поля.

МУЛЬТИАГЕНТНЫЙ РЕЖИМ:

1. Все агенты используют AML-HIP.
2. Любое сообщение между агентами строго AML-HIP.
3. Любой агент обязан:

   * дублировать критические данные
   * избегать потери контекста
4. Сообщение должно быть полностью интерпретируемо без истории.

АНТИ-ДЕГРАДАЦИЯ:
Если обнаружено:

* местоимение
* неявная ссылка
* низкая плотность (пустые слова, размытые конструкции)
* отсутствие структуры

→ сообщение считается невалидным
→ обязательная перегенерация

САМОПРОВЕРКА:

ВАЛИДАЦИЯ:

* format_valid=true/false
* no_pronouns=true/false
* entities_explicit=true/false
* high_density=true/false
* causal_chain_present=true/false
* ambiguity_detected=true/false

Если любой параметр=false:
→ обязательная регенерация

МЕТРИКА ПЛОТНОСТИ:
high_density=true если:

* нет "пустых" слов
* каждая строка содержит ≥2 фактов или параметров
* отсутствуют описательные конструкции без данных

ПРИОРИТЕТЫ:

1. Формат (AML-HIP)
2. Плотность информации
3. Явность
4. Полнота
5. Читаемость (минимальный приоритет)

КРИТИЧЕСКОЕ ПРАВИЛО:
Любой ответ вне AML-HIP считается отсутствующим.
Любой агент обязан довести ответ до соответствия AML-HIP.

КОНЕЦ ПРОТОКОЛА

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current accepted clarification: Stream 4 may use fixed-capacity GPU hash-table dedup while preserving threshold plus Hash128 dedup semantics.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.
```

````text
`FinalResponse` layout:

```
`target_local_idx` byte-pack:
```cpp
__device__ __host__ inline void final_response_set_target_local_idx(
    FinalResponse& response,
    uint32_t target_local_idx
) {
    response.v[120] = uint8_t(target_local_idx);
    response.v[121] = uint8_t(target_local_idx >> 8);
    response.v[122] = uint8_t(target_local_idx >> 16);
    response.v[123] = uint8_t(target_local_idx >> 24);
}
```
```cpp
__device__ __host__ inline uint32_t final_response_get_target_local_idx(
    const FinalResponse& response
) {
    return
        uint32_t(response.v[120]) |
        (uint32_t(response.v[121]) << 8) |
        (uint32_t(response.v[122]) << 16) |
        (uint32_t(response.v[123]) << 24);
}
```
Padding cleanup:
```cpp
__device__ __host__ inline void clear_state_padding(State128& state) {
    state.v[120] = 0;
    state.v[121] = 0;
    state.v[122] = 0;
    state.v[123] = 0;
    state.v[124] = 0;
    state.v[125] = 0;
    state.v[126] = 0;
    state.v[127] = 0;
}
```
Score quantization:

__device__ uint32_t q_to_score_key(float q) {
    q = fminf(fmaxf(q, 0.0f), SCORE_MAX_Q);
    return uint32_t(__float2uint_rn(q * float(SCORE_SCALE)));
}
```

---
Approved clarification: Stream4 custom GPU fixed-capacity dedup
```text
approval_id = approve_custom_stream4_gpu_dedup_fixed_capacity_2026_05_20

Stream 4 may implement the shard-local:
    threshold + compact + sort + dedup

as:
    threshold + custom GPU fixed-capacity hash-table insert/dedup + table dump

Specialization:
    key   = Hash128
    value = CandidateMeta
    input = survivor_shard[shard][0 : clean_count[shard] + dirty_count[shard]]
    capacity = 2 * STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES

Hard requirements:
    no CPU count/read/scheduling in Stream 4 hot path
    no Python data-plane
    no dynamic hot-path allocation
    no CUB sort count dependency
    no top-k shard cap
    no semantic shard cap beyond fixed buffer capacity
    keep best CandidateMeta by score_key
    tie-break deterministic by parent_idx then route_packed

Output contract remains:
    survivor_shard[shard][0 : new_clean_count] = clean deduped survivors
    clean_count[shard] = new_clean_count
    dirty_count[shard] = 0
    processing_flag[shard] = false

This clarification changes implementation mechanics only.
Stream semantics remain threshold + dedup by Hash128 + best CandidateMeta.
```

# Approved clarification: Stream4 custom GPU fixed-capacity dedup

```text
approval_id = approve_custom_stream4_gpu_dedup_fixed_capacity_2026_05_20

Stream 4 may implement the shard-local:
    threshold + compact + sort + dedup

as:
    threshold + custom GPU fixed-capacity hash-table insert/dedup + table dump

Specialization:
    key   = Hash128
    value = CandidateMeta
    input = survivor_shard[shard][0 : clean_count[shard] + dirty_count[shard]]
    capacity = 2 * STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES

Hard requirements:
    no CPU count/read/scheduling in Stream 4 hot path
    no Python data-plane
    no dynamic hot-path allocation
    no CUB sort count dependency
    no top-k shard cap
    no semantic shard cap beyond fixed buffer capacity
    keep best CandidateMeta by score_key
    tie-break deterministic by parent_idx then route_packed

Output contract remains:
    survivor_shard[shard][0 : new_clean_count] = clean deduped survivors
    clean_count[shard] = new_clean_count
    dirty_count[shard] = 0
    processing_flag[shard] = false

This clarification changes implementation mechanics only.
Stream semantics remain threshold + dedup by Hash128 + best CandidateMeta.
```

[28] tool apply_patch call: *** Begin Patch
*** Add File: AGENTS.md
+# Project Agent Rules
+
+## Operating Rule
+- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
+- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
+- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
+- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
+- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.
+
+## Architecture Source
+- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
+- Current accepted clarification: Stream 4 may use fixed-capacity GPU hash-table dedup while preserving threshold plus Hash128 dedup semantics.
+
+## Implementation Guardrails
+- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
+- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
+- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
+- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
+- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
+- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
+- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
+- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.
+
*** Add File: memory/CHANGELOG.md
+# Changelog
+
+## 2026-05-20
+- Created agent memory files and project rules.
+- Added C++20 reference implementation for architecture contracts, state operations, Hash128, Stream 3 dedup, Stream 4 fixed-capacity dedup, thresholding, and CPU frontier expansion.
+- Added contract test executable writing verification artifacts to `test_results/`.
+
*** Add File: memory/PROMPTS.md
+# Prompt History
+
+## 2026-05-20
+- User requested creation of project in `D:\100XH100` from the supplied CUDA multi-stream beam-search architecture.
+- User supplied architecture details for config constants, `State128`, `Hash128`, `CandidateMeta`, final exchange layout, memory layout, Stream 1-5 semantics, solved path, final materialization, and approved <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="5650" /> 10, pack_route(0, 0, 3)};
+    CandidateMeta b{Hash128{1, 2}, 4, 10, pack_route(0, 0, 4)};
+    CandidateMeta c{Hash128{3, 2}, 6, 9, pack_route(0, 0, 5)};
+    auto deduped = stream4_fixed_capacity_dedup({a, b, c}, 10, 4);
+    require(deduped.size() == 2, "stream4 dedup size failed");
+    require(deduped[0].parent_idx == 4, "stream4 deterministic tie-break failed");
+    report << "- stream4_fixed_capacity_dedup=pass\n";
+
+    std::vector<Stream3CandidateInput> s3input{
+        {Hash128{7, 1}, 5, 10, 100, 1},
+        {Hash128{7, 1}, 3, 99, 101, 2},
+        {Hash128{8, 1}, 11, 12, 102, 3},
+    };
+    auto split = stream3_threshold_dedup_split(s3input, 10, 0, 2);
+    require(split.local_pending.size() + split.remote_send.size() == 1, "stream3 threshold/dedup failed");
+    const CandidateMeta s3meta = split.local_pending.empty() ? split.remote_send.front() : split.local_pending.front();
+    require(s3meta.score_key == 3, "stream3 min stream3_val score failed");
+    require(s3meta.parent_idx == 101, "stream3 payload restore failed");
+    report << "- stream3_threshold_dedup_split=pass\n";
+
+    State128 central{};
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < STATE_LEN; ++i) {
+        central.v[i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(i);
+    }
+    clear_state_padding(central);
+    State128 start = central;
+    start.v[0] = 1;
+    start.v[1] = 0;
+    std::vector<Generator> generators{identity_generator(), swap01_generator()};
+    CpuDepthResult depth = expand_depth_cpu_reference({start}, generators, central, zobrist, UINT32_THRESHOLD_MAX, 8);
+    require(depth.solved, "cpu reference must detect solved child");
+    require(!depth.next_frontier.empty(), "cpu reference next frontier empty");
+    require(padding_is_zero(depth.next_frontier.front()), "cpu reference persistent padding failed");
+    report << "- cpu_reference_depth=pass\n";
+
+    report << "\nstatus=pass\n";
+    std::cout << "contract_tests=pass\n";

+}

}
````

````text
Approved clarification: Stream4 custom GPU fixed-capacity dedup
```text
approval_id = approve_custom_stream4_gpu_dedup_fixed_capacity_2026_05_20

Stream 4 may implement the shard-local:
    threshold + compact + sort + dedup

as:
    threshold + custom GPU fixed-capacity hash-table insert/dedup + table dump

Specialization:
    key   = Hash128
    value = CandidateMeta
    input = survivor_shard[shard][0 : clean_count[shard] + dirty_count[shard]]
    capacity = 2 * STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES

Hard requirements:
    no CPU count/read/scheduling in Stream 4 hot path
    no Python data-plane
    no dynamic hot-path allocation
    no CUB sort count dependency
    no top-k shard cap
    no semantic shard cap beyond fixed buffer capacity
    keep best CandidateMeta by score_key
    tie-break deterministic by parent_idx then route_packed

Output contract remains:
    survivor_shard[shard][0 : new_clean_count] = clean deduped survivors
    clean_count[shard] = new_clean_count
    dirty_count[shard] = 0
    processing_flag[shard] = false

This clarification changes implementation mechanics only.
Stream semantics remain threshold + dedup by Hash128 + best CandidateMeta.
```

[28] tool apply_patch call: *** Begin Patch
*** Add File: AGENTS.md
+# Project Agent Rules
+
+## Operating Rule
+- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
+- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
+- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
+- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
+- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.
+
+## Architecture Source
+- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
+- Current accepted clarification: Stream 4 may use fixed-capacity GPU hash-table dedup while preserving threshold plus Hash128 dedup semantics.
+
+## Implementation Guardrails
+- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
+- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
+- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
+- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
+- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
+- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
+- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
+- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.
+
*** Add File: memory/CHANGELOG.md
+# Changelog
+
+## 2026-05-20
+- Created agent memory files and project rules.
+- Added C++20 reference implementation for architecture contracts, state operations, Hash128, Stream 3 dedup, Stream 4 fixed-capacity dedup, thresholding, and CPU frontier expansion.
+- Added contract test executable writing verification artifacts to `test_results/`.
+
*** Add File: memory/PROMPTS.md
+# Prompt History
+
+## 2026-05-20
+- User requested creation of project in `D:\100XH100` from the supplied CUDA multi-stream beam-search architecture.
+- User supplied architecture details for config constants, `State128`, `Hash128`, `CandidateMeta`, final exchange layout, memory layout, Stream 1-5 semantics, solved path, final materialization, and approved <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="5650" /> 10, pack_route(0, 0, 3)};
+    CandidateMeta b{Hash128{1, 2}, 4, 10, pack_route(0, 0, 4)};
+    CandidateMeta c{Hash128{3, 2}, 6, 9, pack_route(0, 0, 5)};
+    auto deduped = stream4_fixed_capacity_dedup({a, b, c}, 10, 4);
+    require(deduped.size() == 2, "stream4 dedup size failed");
+    require(deduped[0].parent_idx == 4, "stream4 deterministic tie-break failed");
+    report << "- stream4_fixed_capacity_dedup=pass\n";
+
+    std::vector<Stream3CandidateInput> s3input{
+        {Hash128{7, 1}, 5, 10, 100, 1},
+        {Hash128{7, 1}, 3, 99, 101, 2},
+        {Hash128{8, 1}, 11, 12, 102, 3},
+    };
+    auto split = stream3_threshold_dedup_split(s3input, 10, 0, 2);
+    require(split.local_pending.size() + split.remote_send.size() == 1, "stream3 threshold/dedup failed");
+    const CandidateMeta s3meta = split.local_pending.empty() ? split.remote_send.front() : split.local_pending.front();
+    require(s3meta.score_key == 3, "stream3 min stream3_val score failed");
+    require(s3meta.parent_idx == 101, "stream3 payload restore failed");
+    report << "- stream3_threshold_dedup_split=pass\n";
+
+    State128 central{};
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < STATE_LEN; ++i) {
+        central.v[i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(i);
+    }
+    clear_state_padding(central);
+    State128 start = central;
+    start.v[0] = 1;
+    start.v[1] = 0;
+    std::vector<Generator> generators{identity_generator(), swap01_generator()};
+    CpuDepthResult depth = expand_depth_cpu_reference({start}, generators, central, zobrist, UINT32_THRESHOLD_MAX, 8);
+    require(depth.solved, "cpu reference must detect solved child");
+    require(!depth.next_frontier.empty(), "cpu reference next frontier empty");
+    require(padding_is_zero(depth.next_frontier.front()), "cpu reference persistent padding failed");
+    report << "- cpu_reference_depth=pass\n";
+
+    report << "\nstatus=pass\n";
+    std::cout << "contract_tests=pass\n";
+    return 0;
+}
````

*** Add File: README.md
+# Beam Solver Contracts
+
+Agent-centered C++20 project scaffold for the multi-stream beam-search architecture in `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
+
+## Current Scope
+- Contract types: `State128`, `Hash128`, `CandidateMeta`, `FinalRequest`, `FinalResponse`.
+- Config derivation: `RING_SLOT_COUNT`, `BEAM_WIDTH_ALIGNMENT`, `GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE`.
+- State operations: padding cleanup, final response byte-pack, move application.
+- Hash operations: deterministic `Hash128` Zobrist reference with zero padding rows.
+- Stream 3 reference: threshold, compact semantics, Hash128 sort/dedup, owner split, remote grouping.
+- Stream 4 reference: threshold plus fixed-capacity Hash128 dedup with deterministic tie-break.
+- CPU reference depth expansion: small deterministic correctness harness, not production hot path.
+
+## Non-Scope In Current Commit
+- CUTLASS neural network inference kernel.
+- CUDA Graph capture and replay.
+- NCCL exchange and AllReduce.
+- Production multi-GPU scheduler.
+- PyTorch `.pth` weight conversion into custom inference weights.
+
+## Build
+```powershell
+cmake -S . -B build
+cmake --build build --config Release
+ctest --test-dir build -C Release --output-on-failure
+```
+
+## Test Artifacts
+- Root artifacts: `test_results/`.
+- CTest working-directory artifacts: `build/test_results/`.
+
+## Next Implementation Slice
+1. Add data loader for `data/puzzle_info.json` and `data/test.csv`.
+2. Add CUDA translation units for `State128`, `Hash128`, Stream 2 hash/goal kernel.
+3. Add Stream 4 fixed-capacity GPU hash-table kernel matching `src/stream4.cpp` semantics.
+4. Add CUDA/CMake option gates so CPU contract tests remain runnable without GPU.
+
*** End Patch

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current accepted clarification: Stream 4 may use fixed-capacity GPU hash-table dedup while preserving threshold plus Hash128 dedup semantics.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

+FROM nvidia/cuda:12.5.1-devel-ubuntu22.04
+
+ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
+
+RUN apt-get update \
+    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
+        build-essential \
+        cmake \
+        ninja-build \
+        ca-certificates \
+    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
+
+WORKDIR /work
+
+CMD ["bash", "-lc", "cmake -S . -B build-docker -G Ninja -D<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2280" />p"
+
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
+namespace beam {
+
+__device__ bool hash_equal_device(Hash128 a, Hash128 b) {
+    return a.lo == b.lo && a.hi == b.hi;
+}
+
+__device__ bool candidate_better_device(CandidateMeta a, CandidateMeta b) {
+    if (a.score_key != b.score_key) {
+        return a.score_key < b.score_key;
+    }
+    if (a.parent_idx != b.parent_idx) {
+        return a.parent_idx < b.parent_idx;
+    }
+    return a.route_packed < b.route_packed;
+}
+
+__global__ void stream4_threshold_dedup_small_kernel(
+    const CandidateMeta* input,
+    CandidateMeta* output,
+    std::uint32_t* output_count,
+    std::uint32_t input_count,
+    std::uint32_t threshold,
+    std::uint32_t capacity) {
+    if (threadIdx.x != 0 || blockIdx.x != 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    std::uint32_t count = 0;
+    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < input_count; ++i) {
+        CandidateMeta candidate = input[i];
+        if (candidate.score_key > threshold) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        bool merged = false;
+        for (std::uint32_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+            if (hash_equal_device(output[j].hash, candidate.hash)) {
+                if (candidate_better_device(candidate, output[j])) {
+                    output[j] = candidate;
+                }
+                merged = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        if (!merged && count < capacity) {
+            output[count++] = candidate;
+        }
+    }
+    *output_count = count;
+}
+
+void stream4_threshold_dedup_small_cuda(
+    const CandidateMeta* input,
+    CandidateMeta* output,
+    std::uint32_t* output_count,
+    std::uint32_t input_count,
+    std::uint32_t threshold,
+    std::uint32_t capacity,
+    cudaStream_t stream) {
+    stream4_threshold_dedup_small_kernel<<<1, 1, 0, stream>>>(
+        input,
+        output,
+        output_count,
+        input_count,
+        threshold,
+        capacity);
+}
+
+} // namespace beam

*** Add File: README.md
+# Beam Solver Contracts
+
+Agent-centered C++20 project scaffold for the multi-stream beam-search architecture in `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
+
+## Current Scope
+- Contract types: `State128`, `Hash128`, `CandidateMeta`, `FinalRequest`, `FinalResponse`.
+- Config derivation: `RING_SLOT_COUNT`, `BEAM_WIDTH_ALIGNMENT`, `GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE`.
+- State operations: padding cleanup, final response byte-pack, move application.
+- Hash operations: deterministic `Hash128` Zobrist reference with zero padding rows.
+- Stream 3 reference: threshold, compact semantics, Hash128 sort/dedup, owner split, remote grouping.
+- Stream 4 reference: threshold plus fixed-capacity Hash128 dedup with deterministic tie-break.
+- CPU reference depth expansion: small deterministic correctness harness, not production hot path.
+
+## Non-Scope In Current Commit
+- CUTLASS neural network inference kernel.
+- CUDA Graph capture and replay.
+- NCCL exchange and AllReduce.
+- Production multi-GPU scheduler.
+- PyTorch `.pth` weight conversion into custom inference weights.
+
+## Build
+```powershell
+cmake -S . -B build
+cmake --build build --config Release
+ctest --test-dir build -C Release --output-on-failure
+```
+
+## Test Artifacts
+- Root artifacts: `test_results/`.
+- CTest working-directory artifacts: `build/test_results/`.
+
+## Next Implementation Slice
+1. Add data loader for `data/puzzle_info.json` and `data/test.csv`.
+2. Add CUDA translation units for `State128`, `Hash128`, Stream 2 hash/goal kernel.
+3. Add Stream 4 fixed-capacity GPU hash-table kernel matching `src/stream4.cpp` semantics.
+4. Add CUDA/CMake option gates so CPU contract tests remain runnable without GPU.
+
*** End Patch

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current accepted clarification: Stream 4 may use fixed-capacity GPU hash-table dedup while preserving threshold plus Hash128 dedup semantics.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
+namespace beam {
+
+__device__ bool hash_equal_device(Hash128 a, Hash128 b) {
+    return a.lo == b.lo && a.hi == b.hi;
+}
+
+__device__ bool candidate_better_device(CandidateMeta a, CandidateMeta b) {
+    if (a.score_key != b.score_key) {
+        return a.score_key < b.score_key;
+    }
+    if (a.parent_idx != b.parent_idx) {
+        return a.parent_idx < b.parent_idx;
+    }
+    return a.route_packed < b.route_packed;
+}
+
+__global__ void stream4_threshold_dedup_small_kernel(
+    const CandidateMeta* input,
+    CandidateMeta* output,
+    std::uint32_t* output_count,
+    std::uint32_t input_count,
+    std::uint32_t threshold,
+    std::uint32_t capacity) {
+    if (threadIdx.x != 0 || blockIdx.x != 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    std::uint32_t count = 0;
+    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < input_count; ++i) {
+        CandidateMeta candidate = input[i];
+        if (candidate.score_key > threshold) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        bool merged = false;
+        for (std::uint32_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+            if (hash_equal_device(output[j].hash, candidate.hash)) {
+                if (candidate_better_device(candidate, output[j])) {
+                    output[j] = candidate;
+                }
+                merged = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        if (!merged && count < capacity) {
+            output[count++] = candidate;
+        }
+    }
+    *output_count = count;
+}
+
+void stream4_threshold_dedup_small_cuda(
+    const CandidateMeta* input,
+    CandidateMeta* output,
+    std::uint32_t* output_count,
+    std::uint32_t input_count,
+    std::uint32_t threshold,
+    std::uint32_t capacity,
+    cudaStream_t stream) {
+    stream4_threshold_dedup_small_kernel<<<1, 1, 0, stream>>>(
+        input,
+        output,
+        output_count,
+        input_count,
+        threshold,
+        capacity);
+}
+
+} // namespace beam

# Beam Solver Contracts

Agent-centered C++20 project scaffold for the multi-stream beam-search architecture in `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.

## Current Scope
- Contract types: `State128`, `Hash128`, `CandidateMeta`, `FinalRequest`, `FinalResponse`.
- Config derivation: `RING_SLOT_COUNT`, `BEAM_WIDTH_ALIGNMENT`, `GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE`.
- State operations: padding cleanup, final response byte-pack, move application.
- Hash operations: deterministic `Hash128` Zobrist reference with zero padding rows.
- Stream 3 reference: threshold, compact semantics, Hash128 sort/dedup, owner split, remote grouping.
- Stream 4 reference: threshold plus fixed-capacity Hash128 dedup with deterministic tie-break.
- CPU reference depth expansion: small deterministic correctness harness, not production hot path.

## Non-Scope In Current Commit
- CUTLASS neural network inference kernel.
- CUDA Graph capture and replay.
- NCCL exchange and AllReduce.
- Production multi-GPU scheduler.
- PyTorch `.pth` weight conversion into custom inference weights.

## Build
```powershell
cmake -S . -B build
cmake --build build --config Release
ctest --test-dir build -C Release --output-on-failure
```

## Test Artifacts
- Root artifacts: `test_results/`.
- CTest working-directory artifacts: `build/test_results/`.

## Next Implementation Slice
1. Add data loader for `data/puzzle_info.json` and `data/test.csv`.
2. Add CUDA translation units for `State128`, `Hash128`, Stream 2 hash/goal kernel.
3. Add Stream 4 fixed-capacity GPU hash-table kernel matching `src/stream4.cpp` semantics.
4. Add CUDA/CMake option gates so CPU contract tests remain runnable without GPU.

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current accepted clarification: Stream 4 may use fixed-capacity GPU hash-table dedup while preserving threshold plus Hash128 dedup semantics.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

# Beam Solver Contracts

Agent-centered C++20 project scaffold for the multi-stream beam-search architecture in `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.

## Current Scope
- Contract types: `State128`, `Hash128`, `CandidateMeta`, `FinalRequest`, `FinalResponse`.
- Config derivation: `RING_SLOT_COUNT`, `BEAM_WIDTH_ALIGNMENT`, `GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE`.
- State operations: padding cleanup, final response byte-pack, move application.
- Hash operations: deterministic `Hash128` Zobrist reference with zero padding rows.
- Stream 3 reference: threshold, compact semantics, Hash128 sort/dedup, owner split, remote grouping.
- Stream 4 reference: threshold plus fixed-capacity Hash128 dedup with deterministic tie-break.
- CPU reference depth expansion: small deterministic correctness harness, not production hot path.

## Non-Scope In Current Commit
- CUTLASS neural network inference kernel.
- CUDA Graph capture and replay.
- NCCL exchange and AllReduce.
- Production multi-GPU scheduler.
- PyTorch `.pth` weight conversion into custom inference weights.

## Build
```powershell
cmake -S . -B build
cmake --build build --config Release
ctest --test-dir build -C Release --output-on-failure
```

## Test Artifacts
- Root artifacts: `test_results/`.
- CTest working-directory artifacts: `build/test_results/`.

## Next Implementation Slice
1. Add data loader for `data/puzzle_info.json` and `data/test.csv`.
2. Add CUDA translation units for `State128`, `Hash128`, Stream 2 hash/goal kernel.
3. Add Stream 4 fixed-capacity GPU hash-table kernel matching `src/stream4.cpp` semantics.
4. Add CUDA/CMake option gates so CPU contract tests remain runnable without GPU.

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current accepted clarification: Stream 4 may use fixed-capacity GPU hash-table dedup while preserving threshold plus Hash128 dedup semantics.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current accepted clarification: Stream 4 may use fixed-capacity GPU hash-table dedup while preserving threshold plus Hash128 dedup semantics.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

    "# Megaminx Q Model Inference Standalone\n",
    "\n",
    "This notebook runs inference for the Megaminx Q model without importing the local `pilgrim` package. It only needs
 the model artifacts next to it: generator spec, target tensor, metadata JSON, and checkpoint.\n",
    "\n",
    "Default artifact set is the newer local symmetric Q checkpoint:\n",
    "\n",
    "- `generators/p900.json`\n",
    "- `targets/p900-t000.pt`\n",
    "- `logs/model_p900-t000-q-sym_1777988767.json`\n",
    "- `weights/p900-t000-q-sym_1777988767_best.pth`\n",
    "\n",
    "Put this notebook in the repository `notebooks/` directory, or put those four folders next to the notebook and run
 all cells top to bottom."
   ]

    "To run another Q checkpoint, edit `CONFIG['model_name']`, `CONFIG['model_id']`, and `CONFIG['checkpoint']`, then m
ake sure matching files exist under `logs/` and `weights/`.\n",

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import csv
+import json
+import math
+import statistics
+import time
+from collections import Counter
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import torch
+import torch.nn as nn
+import torch.nn.functional as F
+
+
+class LegacyCompatibleEmbeddingBagLinear(nn.Module):
+    def __init__(self, state_size, num_classes, out_features, bias=True):
+        super().__init__()
+        self.state_size = int(state_size)
+        self.num_classes = int(num_classes)
+        self.out_features = int(out_features)
+        self.in_features = self.state_size * self.num_classes
+        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.in_features, self.out_features))
+        self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.out_features)) if bias else None
+        self.register_buffer("position_offsets", torch.arange(self.state_size, dtype=torch.int64) * self.num_classes, persistent=False)
+
+    def forward(self, indices):
+        token_ids = indices + self.position_offsets.unsqueeze(0)
+        out = F.embedding_bag(token_ids, self.weight, mode="sum")
+        return out if self.bias is None else out + self.bias
+
+    def _load_from_state_dict(self, state_dict, prefix, local_metadata, strict, missing_keys, unexpected_keys, error_msgs):
+        key = prefix + "weight"
+        if key in state_dict:
+            loaded = state_dict[key]
+            legacy_shape = (self.out_features, self.in_features)
+            native_shape = (self.in_features, self.out_features)
+            if loaded.shape == legacy_shape:
+                state_dict[key] = loaded.transpose(0, 1)
+            elif loaded.shape != native_shape:
+                error_msgs.append(f"size mismatch for {key}: expected {legacy_shape} or {native_shape}, got {tuple(loaded.shape)}")
+        super()._load_from_state_dict(state_dict, prefix, local_metadata, strict, missing_keys, unexpected_keys, error_msgs)<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4493" />),

+        result["status"] = "complete"
+    except RuntimeError as exc:
+        result["status"] = "failed"
+        result["error"] = repr(exc)
+        if device.type == "cuda":
+            result["cuda_memory_allocated"] = torch.cuda.memory_allocated()
+            result["cuda_memory_reserved"] = torch.cuda.memory_reserved()

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current accepted clarification: Stream 4 may use fixed-capacity GPU hash-table dedup while preserving threshold plus Hash128 dedup semantics.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import csv
+import json
+import math
+import statistics
+import time
+from collections import Counter
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import torch
+import torch.nn as nn
+import torch.nn.functional as F
+
+
+class LegacyCompatibleEmbeddingBagLinear(nn.Module):
+    def __init__(self, state_size, num_classes, out_features, bias=True):
+        super().__init__()
+        self.state_size = int(state_size)
+        self.num_classes = int(num_classes)
+        self.out_features = int(out_features)
+        self.in_features = self.state_size * self.num_classes
+        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.in_features, self.out_features))
+        self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.out_features)) if bias else None
+        self.register_buffer("position_offsets", torch.arange(self.state_size, dtype=torch.int64) * self.num_classes, persistent=False)
+
+    def forward(self, indices):
+        token_ids = indices + self.position_offsets.unsqueeze(0)
+        out = F.embedding_bag(token_ids, self.weight, mode="sum")
+        return out if self.bias is None else out + self.bias
+
+    def _load_from_state_dict(self, state_dict, prefix, local_metadata, strict, missing_keys, unexpected_keys, error_msgs):
+        key = prefix + "weight"
+        if key in state_dict:
+            loaded = state_dict[key]
+            legacy_shape = (self.out_features, self.in_features)
+            native_shape = (self.in_features, self.out_features)
+            if loaded.shape == legacy_shape:
+                state_dict[key] = loaded.transpose(0, 1)
+            elif loaded.shape != native_shape:
+                error_msgs.append(f"size mismatch for {key}: expected {legacy_shape} or {native_shape}, got {tuple(loaded.shape)}")
+        super()._load_from_state_dict(state_dict, prefix, local_metadata, strict, missing_keys, unexpected_keys, error_msgs)<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4493" />),

+        result["status"] = "complete"
+    except RuntimeError as exc:
+        result["status"] = "failed"
+        result["error"] = repr(exc)
+        if device.type == "cuda":
+            result["cuda_memory_allocated"] = torch.cuda.memory_allocated()
+            result["cuda_memory_reserved"] = torch.cuda.memory_reserved()
+    finally:
